                    $(this).css({
                        height: height
                    });
                }
            });
        }
    });

                } else {
                    $('.newsLetterForm').html(data.error);
                }
            });
        }
    });

    //For Desktop price range

            parentDomain = "";
        }
        else {
            parentDomain = "https://www.gabrielny.com";

        var url = parentDomain + "/engagement-rings/price-" + menu_price_low + "/advanced-false";

        window.location.href = url;

    });

});

                    $('#newsLetterForm').html(data.error);

    $(".search-box .btnSearchMob").on('click', function (e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        e.preventDefault();
        var textBox = $('.search-box .itemSearchMob').val();
        if (!textBox || typeof textBox === undefined || textBox === null)
        {
            $('.search-box .itemSearchMob').focus();
        }
        else
        {
            searchURL(e, null, textBox);
        }
    });

    $(".search-box .btnSearchEmbed").on('click', function (e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        e.preventDefault();
        var textBox = $('.search-box .itemSearch').val();
        if (!textBox || typeof textBox === undefined || textBox === null)
        {
            $('.search-box .itemSearch').focus();
        }
        else
        {
            searchURL(e, null, textBox);
        }
    });

    $(document.body).on('keypress', '.search-box .itemSearch', function (event) {
        event.stopPropagation();
        if (event.which == 13) {
            event.preventDefault();
            searchURL(event, this);
        }
    });
	/* Top Search Box End */

});

function loadCurrencyEngine() {
    $('.currencySelect').on('click', function (e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        e.preventDefault();
        var currencyValue = $(this).data('value');
        var currencyType = $(this).data('type');
        var currencyDelim = $(this).data('delim');
        var changeElement = $(this).data('update');
        $.ajax({
            url: '/ajax/_global/converCurrency',
            dataType: 'json',
            async: false,
            data: {'currencyValue': currencyValue, 'currencyType': currencyType},
            success: function (data) {
                if (data.status == 'success') {
                    $('#' + changeElement).html(data.message);
                    $('#' + currencyDelim).html(currencyType);
                } else {
                    alert(data.message);
                }
            }
        });
    });
}

var IcarusRedirect = new (function() {
    this.now = function (url) {
        document.location.href = url;
    };

    this.soon = function (url, time) {
        setTimeout(function() {
            IcarusRedirect.now(url);
        }, time);
    }
})();

function passwordStrength(password) {
    var desc = new Array();
    desc[0] = "Very Weak (0)";
    desc[1] = "Weak (1)";
    desc[2] = "Better (2)";
    desc[3] = "Medium (3)";
    desc[4] = "Strong (4)";
    desc[5] = "Strongest (5)";
    var score = 0;

    //if password bigger than 6 give 1 point
    if (password.length > 6) score++;

    //if password has both lower and uppercase characters give 1 point
    if (( password.match(/[a-z]/) ) && ( password.match(/[A-Z]/) )) score++;

    //if password has at least one number give 1 point
    if (password.match(/\d+/)) score++;

    //if password has at least one special caracther give 1 point
    if (password.match(/.[!,@,#,$,%,^,&,*,?,_,~,-,(,)]/)) score++;

    //if password bigger than 12 give another 1 point
    if (password.length > 12) score++;
    document.getElementById("passwordDescription").innerHTML = desc[score];
    document.getElementById("passwordStrength").className = "strength" + score;
}

function getPasswordLevel(password) {
    var score = 0;

    //if password bigger than 6 give 1 point
    if (password.length > 6) score++;

    //if password has both lower and uppercase characters give 1 point
    if (( password.match(/[a-z]/) ) && ( password.match(/[A-Z]/) )) score++;

    //if password has at least one number give 1 point
    if (password.match(/\d+/)) score++;

    //if password has at least one special caracther give 1 point
    if (password.match(/.[!,@,#,$,%,^,&,*,?,_,~,-,(,)]/)) score++;

    //if password bigger than 12 give another 1 point
    if (password.length > 12) score++;

    return score;
}

    var HighLightSwitchoption=function(){

        $('.product').each(function(i){
            var prod_id = $(this).data('id');
            var tmp_prod_id=$('#product'+prod_id).data("id");      

               //Metal               
                $('#product'+prod_id+' .product_show .product-metal li').each(function(){                                          
                    if(tmp_prod_id==$(this).find('a').attr('data-color')){                              
                        $(this).addClass('active');
                        if($(this).find('img').attr("data-imgsel")!=""){
                            $(this).find('img').attr('src',$(this).find('img').attr("data-imgsel"));
                        }
                    }
                });

                //Shape
                $('#product'+prod_id+' .product_show .product-shape li').each(function(){                                                
                    if(tmp_prod_id==$(this).find('a').attr('data-color')){                       
                        $(this).addClass('active');                        
                        if($(this).find('img').attr("data-imgsel")!=""){
                            $(this).find('img').attr('src',$(this).find('img').attr("data-imgsel"));
                        }
                    }
                });

                //Stone
                $('#product'+prod_id+' .product_show .product-stone li').each(function(){                      
                    if(tmp_prod_id==$(this).find('a').attr('data-color')){                       
                        $(this).addClass('active');
                    }
                });

                //Size
                $('#product'+prod_id+' .product-overlay .product-size li').each(function(){                      
                    if(tmp_prod_id==$(this).find('a').attr('data-color')){                       
                        $(this).addClass('active');
                    }
                });

                //TDW
                $('#product'+prod_id+' .product-overlay .product-total-diamond li').each(function(){
                    if(tmp_prod_id==$(this).find('a').attr('data-color')){
                        $(this).addClass('active');
                    }
                });
         });
    }
    HighLightSwitchoption();

    //Change Metal and Shape images when hover.
    var optionImgHighlight = function (){
        $(".option-image-container").mouseover(function () {      
          var checkcls=$(this).parent().parent().hasClass('active');
          var imagehover=$(this).data("imghov");          
          if(!checkcls){
             if(imagehover!=""){                
                $(this).attr('src',imagehover);
             }
          }      
        }).mouseout(function () {     
          var checkcls=$(this).parent().parent().hasClass('active');
          var imagehover=$(this).data("imghov");
          var imageorg=$(this).data("imgorg");
          if(!checkcls){
            if(imageorg!=""){                
                $(this).attr('src',imageorg);
             }
          }      
        });
    }
    optionImgHighlight();

        });

        e.preventDefault();

        $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
        $('#modal-add-to-favorite').remove();
        $('#modal-move-to-favorite').remove();

        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: '/ajax/account/favorites',
            data: "mode=getStatus&item=" + $(this).attr('data-code'),
            dataType: "json",
            success: function (data) {
                $('body').append($(data.html).modal());
            }
        });
    });
    //===========================End Set Favorites==============================//

    //Start Display Extra Images
    $('.product-extra ul>li').off('click').on('click', function(e) {
        var item = $(this).data('item');
        var currentItem=$(this).data('current');
        var id = $(this).data('id');
        var baseImgElement = $('#product'+currentItem);

        if(id>0){
            url = "https://s7d4.scene7.com/is/image/GabrielCo/" + item + "-"+id+"?$Medium$";
            baseImgElement.find('.imgcontainer').attr('data-itemImage',url);
            baseImgElement.find('.itemListingImage').attr({"src": url});
        }
    });
    //End Display Extra Images

});

        $('.btnFindStorePop').unbind('click').bind('click', function (e)
        {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
			/*
			 pintrk​(​'track'​,​ ​'storesearch'​,​ {
			 lead_type​:​ ​'Search For Store'
			 ​});
			 */
            var division=$(this).attr('data-division');
            var sku=$(this).attr('data-item');


            if(!!$.cookie('{MyCookiePrefix}MY_LOCATION'))
            {
                $.ajax({
                    type: 'POST',
                    url: '/ajax/_global/mylocation',
                    data: 'mode=getlocation',
                    dataType: 'json',
                    success: function(data) {
                        console.log('Loading....', data);
                        if (data.status == 'OK') {
                            window.location = 'https://www.gabrielny.com/schedule-a-private-show/results?productId='+sku+'&division='+division+'&lat='+data.lat+'&lng='+data.lng;
                        } else {
                            console.log('Unable to read cookie.');
                            if (navigator.geolocation)
                            {
                                var self = this;
                                navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(function(position) {
                                    showMyPositionLocal(position, self, sku, division)
                                }, showMyErrorLocal);
                            } else {
                                alert("Your browser does not support geo location.");
                            }
                        }
                    },
                    error: function() {
                        //alert('Error occured');
                    }
                });

            }
            else
            {
                if (navigator.geolocation)
                {
                    var self = this;
                    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(function(position) {
                        showMyPositionLocal(position, self, sku, division)
                    }, showMyErrorLocal);
                } else {
                    alert("Your browser does not support geo location.");
                }
            }



            //document.location.href = 'https://www.gabrielny.com/storelocator/?eshopMode='+division+'&sku='+sku;
        });

        function showMyPositionLocal(position, self, sku, division)
        {
            var lat = position.coords.latitude;
            var lng = position.coords.longitude;
            $("#loader").show();
            $.post("/ajax/_global/mylocation/", {lat:position.coords.latitude,lng:position.coords.longitude},
                function(data)
                {
                    window.location = 'https://www.gabrielny.com/schedule-a-private-show/results?productId='+sku+'&division='+division+'&lat='+lat+'&lng='+lng;
                });
        }

        function showMyErrorLocal(error) {
            var errorCode = "";
            switch(error.code) {
                case error.PERMISSION_DENIED:
                    errorCode = "User denied the request for Geolocation.";
                    break;
                case error.POSITION_UNAVAILABLE:
                    errorCode = "Location information is unavailable.";
                    break;
                case error.TIMEOUT:
                    errorCode = "The request to get user location timed out.";
                    break;
                case error.UNKNOWN_ERROR:
                    errorCode = "An unknown error occurred.";
                    break;
            }
            alert(errorCode);
        }

function getQuantity(navName) {
    function success(data) {
        if (data) {
            var elem = "<div id='salestock' class='fg-red heading not-in-stock'>Not In Stock<i class='icon-cancel-2 on-right fg-red'></i></div>";
            if (data == "yes") {
                elem = "<div id='salestock' class='fg-green heading in-stock'>In Stock<i class='icon-checkmark on-right fg-green'></i></div>";
            }
            $("#stockstatus").html(elem);
        }
    }

    $.post("/ajax/eshop/quantity?product=" + navName + "&mode=check", success);
    $.post("/ajax/eshop/quantity?product=" + navName + "&mode=num", function (data) {
        $(".stocknum").html(data);
    });
}

function processScreen() {
    return;
    $('#prodImage, #prodImageMobile').removeData('elevateZoom');
    $('.zoomContainer').remove();
}

function searchURL(e, elem, term) {
    if (!term || typeof term === undefined || term === null) term = $(elem).val();

    if (term && term.length > 1) {
        term = encodeURIComponent(term);
        var locationUrl=$(location).attr('href');
        if(locationUrl.indexOf('fashion-stackable')!==-1 || locationUrl.indexOf('eternity-stackable')!==-1){
            var redirectUrl="https://www.gabrielny.com";
            if(locationUrl.indexOf('fashion-stackable')!==-1){
                redirectUrl=redirectUrl+'/fashion-stackable';
            }
            if(locationUrl.indexOf('eternity-stackable')!==-1){
                redirectUrl=redirectUrl+'/eternity-stackable';
            }
            gotoStackableFilter(redirectUrl,term);
        }else{
            gotoAdvanceFilter(term);
        }
    }
}

function gotoStackableFilter(locationUrl,term){
    //console.log(locationUrl+'/searchkeywordstring-'+term)
    $('#loader').show();
    window.location.href = locationUrl+'/searchkeywordstring-'+term;
}

function gotoAdvanceFilter(term) {
    //debugger;

    term = term.toLowerCase();
    term = term.replace(/\%2f|\%5c/g,'');
    // Ticket #2741 - Allow Searches for "Serial Number" on website Start
    var serialSearch = false;
    var w = document.body.clientWidth;
	if (w > 991)
	{
		if($('.top_search_desk #topSearchBySerial').is(":checked")) {
			serialSearch = true;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		if($('.top_search_mob #topSearchBySerial').is(":checked")) {
			serialSearch = true;
		}
	}

    if(serialSearch)
    {
        if (window.location.host.indexOf("embed") >= 0) {

        } else {
            var serialNo = term;
            $.ajax({
                type: 'POST',
                dataType: 'json',
                url: '/ajax/eshop/getstylenofromserialno',
                data: {serialNo: serialNo},
                async: true
            }).done(function(data)
            {
				
                if(data.success)
                {

                    if(data.specialItem == '1' || data.itemStatusFilter == 'Discontinued')
                    {
                        var url = "https://www.gabrielny.com/discontinued-items";
                        window.location.href = url;
                        return;
                    }
                    else if(data.itemCode != '')
                    {
                        term = data.itemCode;
                        var url = "https://www.gabrielny.com/products-advanced/searchkeywordstring-" + term;                        
                        window.location.href = url;
                        return;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    var url = "https://www.gabrielny.com/products-advanced/searchkeywordstring-" + term;
                    window.location.href = url;
                    return;
                }
            });
        }
    }
    else
    {
        var parentDomain;
        if (window.location.host.indexOf("embed") >= 0) {
            parentDomain = "";
        }
        else {
            parentDomain = "https://www.gabrielny.com";
        }
        var url = parentDomain + "/products-advanced/searchkeywordstring-" + term;

        window.location.href = url;
    }
}

function postsize() {

    var height = 0;
    // var body = document.body, html = document.documentElement;
    // height = Math.max( body.scrollHeight, body.offsetHeight, html.clientHeight, html.scrollHeight, html.offsetHeight );
    //var body = document.body, html = document.documentElement, height = Math.max(body.scrollHeight, body.offsetHeight, html.clientHeight, html.scrollHeight, html.offsetHeight);
    // var height = $("body").outerHeight(true);
    // var height = $("html").height();
    //var height = document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].scrollHeight;
    height =  $("html").outerHeight();
    window.parent.postMessage(["setHeight", height], "*");
    // console.log("height posted " + height);
}


function postFavorites($item) {

    window.parent.postMessage(["setFavorite", $item], "*");
    console.log("favorite posted " + $item);
}



function appendPathToURI(path, url) {
    if (url) {
        if (url.indexOf('?') > 0) {
            var sep = url.split('?');
            var uLen = (sep[0] + '').length;
            if (sep[0] && (sep[0] + '').lastIndexOf('/') == (uLen - 1)) {
                sep[0] = sep[0].substring(0, (uLen - 1));
            }
            url = sep[0] + path;
            if (sep.length > 1) {
                url += '?' + sep[1];
            }
        } else {
            url += path;
        }
    }
    return url;
}

var ESHOP_CONFIG = {
    isEmbed: function () {
        try {
            return window.self !== window.top;
        } catch (e) {
            return true;
        }
    },
    embedOrigin: '{HTTP_REFERER}'
};

window.addEventListener("message", function (message) {
    if (ESHOP_CONFIG && ESHOP_CONFIG.isEmbed() && message && message.data) {
        if (message.data.search && typeof message.data === "object" && typeof message.data.search === "string") {
            console.log(message);
            searchURL(null, null, message.data.search);
        }
        else if (message.data.origin) {
            ESHOP_CONFIG['embedOrigin'] = message.data.origin;
            alert('origin: ' + message.data.origin);
        }
        //alert (JSON.stringify(message.data));
    }
});

jQuery.uaMatch = function (ua) {
    ua = ua.toLowerCase();

    var match = /(chrome)[ /]([w.]+)/.exec(ua) ||
        /(webkit)[ /]([w.]+)/.exec(ua) ||
        /(opera)(?:.*version|)[ /]([w.]+)/.exec(ua) ||
        /(msie) ([w.]+)/.exec(ua) ||
        ua.indexOf("compatible") < 0 && /(mozilla)(?:.*? rv:([w.]+)|)/.exec(ua) ||
        [];

    return {
        browser: match[1] || "",
        version: match[2] || "0"
    };
};


function LoadSmallImage(){

    $('.product-extra ul>li').off('click').on('click', function(e) {

        var item = $(this).data('item');
        var currentItem=$(this).data('current');
        var id = $(this).data('id');
        var baseImgElement = $('#product'+currentItem);

        if(id>0){
            url = "https://s7d4.scene7.com/is/image/GabrielCo/" + item + "-"+id+"?$Medium$";
            baseImgElement.find('.itemListingImage').attr({"src": url});
        }
    });
}

// Don't clobber any existing jQuery.browser in case it's different
if (!jQuery.browser) {
    matched = jQuery.uaMatch(navigator.userAgent);
    browser = {};

    if (matched.browser) {
        browser[matched.browser] = true;
        browser.version = matched.version;
    }

    // Chrome is Webkit, but Webkit is also Safari.
    if (browser.chrome) {
        browser.webkit = true;
    }
    else if (browser.webkit) {
        browser.safari = true;
    }

    jQuery.browser = browser;
};

function requestClearCache(object) {
    var $that = $(object);

    console.log($that.data('path'), $that.data('uri'));

    if (confirm("Are you sure you wish to delete any cached files for:\r\n" + $that.data('path') + "?")) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: '/ajax/_global/clear-cache',
            data: {uri: $that.data('uri')},
            dataType: "json",
            success: function(data) {
                if (data.success) {
                    location.reload();
                }
            }
        });
    }
}


$(function() {

    //var gRDD = ($('#global_Retailer_DD').length > 0);
    var gRDD = ($('.global_Retailer_DD').length > 0);

    //if (gRDD && $('#global_Retailer_DD').is(':visible')) {
    if (gRDD && $('.global_Retailer_DD').is(':visible')) {

        //$('#global_Retailer_DD').autocomplete({
           $('.global_Retailer_DD').autocomplete({
            serviceUrl:"/ajax/_global/staff_company",
            minChars:2,
            dataType: 'json',
            maxHeight:300,
            //width:'100%',
            maxWidth:300,
            triggerSelectOnValidInput: false,
            formatResult : function (suggestion, currentValue)
            {
                var pattern = '(' + (currentValue.replace(/[\-\[\]\/\{\}\(\)\*\+\?\.\\\^\$\|]/g, "\\$&")) + ')';
                if(!suggestion.combo) suggestion.combo = suggestion.value;
                return suggestion.combo.replace(new RegExp(pattern, 'gi'), '<strong>$1<\/strong>');
            },
            transformResult: function(response) {
                return {
                    suggestions: $.map(response, function(dataItem) {
                        $val = dataItem.retCode+" - "+dataItem.retName;
                        return { value: dataItem.retName, combo: $val, data: dataItem };
                    })
                };
            },
            onSearchStart: function()
            {
            },
            onSearchComplete: function() {
            },
            onSelect: function (suggestion)
            {
                $(this).val(suggestion.data.retName);

                if( suggestion.data.retCode !='');
                {
                    var querystr = "global_Retailer_Code="+suggestion.data.retCode;
                    $.post("/ajax/_global/staff_retailer", querystr, function (data)
                    {
                        data = JSON.parse(data);
                        if(data.success)
                        {
                            location.reload();
                        }

                    });
                }

            }
        });

    }

});

$(document).ready(function () {

    $('#retailer_box .btnchangeauthstore, #retailer_box2 .btnchangeauthstore, #retailer_box_mobile .btnchangeauthstore, #serviceProvider .btnchangeauthstore, .btnchangeauthstore').on('click', function(e)
    {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        $("#loader").show();
        $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
        $('#modal-change-store').remove();
        var productId = $('#widgetProductId').val();
        var division = $('#widgetDivision').val();
        var zipcode = $('#widgetZip').val();

        //Facebook Track
        if (typeof fbq == 'function') {
            fbq('track', 'Lead', {content_name: zipcode});
            console.log("Facebook Lead Ping: " + zipcode);
        }
        //End Facebook

        if(zipcode === 'undefined' || zipcode === '') {
            zipcode = $('#widgetZip_mobile').val();
        }
        if (division === 'undefined' || division === '') {
            division = $('#widgetDivision_mobile').val();
        }
        if (productId  === 'undefined' || productId === ''){
            productId = $('#widgetProductId_mobile').val();
        }
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: '/ajax/_global/change-store',
            data: "mode=changeStore&productId="+productId+'&division='+division+'&widgetZip='+zipcode,
            dataType: "json",
            success: function(data)
            {
                $('body').append($(data.html).modal('show'));
                $("#loader").hide();
                initChangeStore();
                return false;
            }
        }).done(function(data) {
            $("#loader").hide();
        });
        $(".modal-content").hide();
    });

    getSLWidgetStockStatus();	//get Stock avaibility status for store locator widget

});

function changeStoreModel() {
    $('#modal-change-store').modal('hide');
    $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
    $('#modal-change-store').remove();
    $("#loader").hide();
}


function initChangeStore()
{
    //hide retailer logo if not exist
	/*$('#retailer_change_box_inner .rectangle').each(function(i, obj) {
	 if(!$(this).find('img').length){
	 $(this).hide();
	 }
	 });*/

    $('#modal-change-store .btnpickauthstore').unbind('click').on('click', function(e)
    {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        $("#loader").show();
        $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
        $('#modal-change-store').remove();

        var retCode = $(this).data('retcode');
        var retName = $(this).data('retname');
        var addName = $(this).data('addname');
        var addAddress1 = $(this).data('addaddress1');
        var tierTitle = $(this).data('tiertitle');
        var address = $(this).data('address');
        var addLat = $(this).data('addlat');
        var addLong = $(this).data('addlong');
        var addAppointment = $(this).data('addappointment');
        var addEmail = $(this).data('addemail');
        var addImage = $(this).data('addimage');
        var shiptostore = $(this).data('shiptostore');

        var eshopMode = $(this).data('mode');
        var pathname = window.location.href;
        //console.log(pathname);
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: '/ajax/_global/change-store',
            data: "mode=setStore&retCode="+retCode+'&retName='+retName+'&addName='+addName+'&addAddress1='+addAddress1+'&tierTitle='+tierTitle+'&address='+address+'&addLat='+addLat+'&addLong='+addLong+'&addAppointment='+addAppointment+'&addEmail='+addEmail+'&addImage='+addImage+'&eshopMode='+eshopMode+'&shiptostore='+shiptostore,
            dataType: "json",
            success: function(data)
            {
                if(data.status == 'OK')
                {
                    if (pathname.indexOf('cart') > -1) {
                        $.redirect(pathname, {'review':1});
                    }else{
                        location.reload();
                    }
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }).done(function(data) {
            $("#loader").hide();
        });
    });

    $("#findauthstore").off('submit').on("submit", function(){

        var Widgetzip = $('#widgetZipLocal').val();
        var Widgetmode = $('#Widgetmode').val();
        var WidgetProductId = $('#widgetProductId').val();

        if(Widgetzip == '')
        {
            alert("Please enter location.");
            $('#Widgetzip').focus();
            return false;
        }

        $("#loader").show();

        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: '/ajax/_global/change-store',
            data: "mode=findStore&widgetZip="+Widgetzip+'&division='+Widgetmode+'&productId='+WidgetProductId,
            dataType: "json",
            success: function(data)
            {
                if(data.status == 'OK')
                {
                    $('#retailer_change_box_inner').html(data.html);
                    //location.reload();
                    $("#loader").hide();
                    initChangeStore();
                    return false;
                }
                else
                {
                    $("#loader").hide();
                    alert('Sorry, something went wrong: Please try again.');
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }).done(function(data) {
            $("#loader").hide();
        });

        return false;
    });

    // $('#modal-change-store .gcny-retailer-stock').each(function(i, obj) {

    //	var retCode = $(this).attr("data-retCode");
    // 	var productId = $(this).attr("data-productId");

    // 		}
    // 		aobj.html(elem);
    // 	});
    // });

}

//get Stock avaibility status for store locator widget
function getSLWidgetStockStatus()
{
    $('#retailer_box .gcny-retailer-stock, #retailer_box2 .gcny-retailer-stock, #retailer_box_mobile .gcny-retailer-stock, #serviceProvider .gcny-retailer-stock').each(function(i, obj) {

        var retCode = $(this).attr("data-retCode");
        var productId = $(this).attr("data-productId");
        if(productId == '')
        {
            productId = '{navName}';
        }
        var aobj = $(this);

        $.ajax({
            type: 'POST',
            dataType: 'json',
            url: '/ajax/_global/store-widget-stock-status',
            data: {retCode: retCode,productId: productId},
            async: true
        }).done(function(data)
        {
            var elem = "View - <span class=\"tcolor4\">7 Days</span>";
            if(data.success)
            {
                if(data.result != '')
                {
                    elem = data.result;
                    //alert(data.days);
                    $('#widgetViewInDays').val(data.days);
                }
                else
                {
                    // elem = 'Available to view at <br/>' + data.retName;
                    elem = 'Available to view at our Gabriel Authorized Independent Retailer  <br/>';
                }
            }
            else
            {
                $('#widgetViewInDays').val(data.days);
                // elem = 'Available to view at <br/>' + data.retName;
                elem = 'Available to view at our Gabriel Authorized Independent Retailer <br/>';
            }
            aobj.html(elem);
        });
    });
}

/* Expand searchbox on click */

$(document).ready(function(){
	var submitIcon = $('.newsearchbox-icon');
	var inputBox = $('.newsearchbox-input');
	var searchBox = $('.newsearchbox');
	var isOpen = false;
	submitIcon.click(function(e){
		var inputVal = $('.newsearchbox-input').val();
		inputVal = $.trim(inputVal).length;
		if( inputVal !== 0){
			e.stopPropagation();
			e.preventDefault();
			var textBox = $('.search-box .itemSearch').val();
			if (!textBox || typeof textBox === undefined || textBox === null)
			{
				$('.search-box .itemSearch').focus();
			}
			else
			{
				searchURL(e, null, textBox);
			}
			return false;
		}
		
		if(isOpen == false){
			searchBox.addClass('newsearchbox-open');
			inputBox.focus();
			isOpen = true;
			$('.top_checkboxes').show();
		} else {
			searchBox.removeClass('newsearchbox-open');
			inputBox.focusout();
			isOpen = false;
			$('.top_checkboxes').hide();
		}
	});  
	submitIcon.mouseup(function(){
			return false;
		});
	searchBox.mouseup(function(){
			return false;
		});
	$(document).mouseup(function(e){
			if(isOpen == true){
				if (!$(e.target).closest('.top_checkboxes').length)
				{
					$('.newsearchbox-icon').css('display','block');
					submitIcon.click();
				}
			}
		});
});

// View In Store Start
function trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents(eventCategory, eventAction, eventLabel, eventValue)
{
    //console.log('trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents - '+eventCategory+' - '+eventAction+' - '+eventLabel);
    if(eventLabel != '' && eventLabel != undefined){
        //var dataLayer = dataLayer || [];
        dataLayer.push({
             'event': 'GeoLocFindStoreBookAppointment',
             'eventCategory': eventCategory,
             'eventAction': eventAction,
             'eventLabel': eventLabel
        });
        //console.log(dataLayer);
    }

}

if('1' == '1')
{
$(function() {  

    
    function showMyPositionLocalNew(position, self, callback)
    {
        var lat = position.coords.latitude;
        var lng = position.coords.longitude;
        //var lat = '40.761';
        //var lng = '-73.330';
        $.post("/ajax/_global/user_location/", {lat:lat,lng:lng, mode: 'set_my_geo_coords'},
            function(data) {
                data = JSON.parse(data);
                if(data.status == "OK") {
                    callback(data);
                }
            });
    }
    
    function showMyErrorLocal(error) {
        var errorCode = "";
        switch(error.code) {
            case error.PERMISSION_DENIED:
                errorCode = "User denied the request for Geolocation.";
                break;
            case error.POSITION_UNAVAILABLE:
                errorCode = "Location information is unavailable.";
                break;
            case error.TIMEOUT:
                errorCode = "The request to get user location timed out.";
                break;
            case error.UNKNOWN_ERROR:
                errorCode = "An unknown error occurred.";
                break;
        }
        alert(errorCode);
    }
    

    // Your Location Popup Start
    var prevent_track_confirm_btn_click = 'no';
    if($("#show_location_popup").val() == 1)
    {
        
        $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: '/ajax/_global/user_location',
                data: "mode=show_location_popup",
                dataType: "json",
                success: function(data)
                {
                    if (data.status == "OK") {
                        $('body').append(data.html);
                        var w = document.body.clientWidth;
                        if (w > 991) {
                            if($('#top_location_popup').length) {
                                $('#top_location_popup').appendTo("body").modal('show');
                                $('#prddtl-find-store #findStoreZip').val($('#top_location_popup #userloc_td_zip').val());
                                $('.find_sop #zip').val($('#top_location_popup #userloc_td_zip').val());
                                $('.find_store #zip').val($('#top_location_popup #userloc_td_zip').val());
                                trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Geo-Location Popup', 'impression', $('#top_location_popup #userloc_td_zip').val());
                                trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Store Locator Leads', 'web traffic', $('#top_location_popup #userloc_td_zip').val());
                                
                            }
                        } else {
                            if($('#top_location_popup_outer').length) {
                                $("#top_location_popup_outer").addClass("sideNavMob");
                                $(".toploc_section .sidenavbg").show();
                                $('#prddtl-find-store #findStoreZip').val($('#toploc_section #userloc_tm_zip').val());
                                $('.find_sop #zip').val($('#toploc_section #userloc_tm_zip').val());
                                $('.find_store #zip').val($('#toploc_section #userloc_tm_zip').val());
                                trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Geo-Location Popup', 'impression', $('#toploc_section #userloc_tm_zip').val());
                                trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Store Locator Leads', 'web traffic', $('#toploc_section #userloc_tm_zip').val());
                                
                            }
                        }
                        $("#show_location_popup").val(0);
                        
                        var uloc_device = 'desk';
                        if (w > 991) {
                            uloc_device = 'desk';
                        } else {
                            uloc_device = 'mob';
                        }
                        productPageMobileScrollSetting(uloc_device);
                    }
                }
            }).done(function(data) {
                
            });

    $(document).on('hidden.bs.modal', function (event) {

    $('body').on('click', '#userloc_td_confirm_btn, #userloc_tm_confirm_btn', function (el){
        var clckd_elm_id = $(this).attr('id');
        var userloc_td_zip = '';
        if(clckd_elm_id == 'userloc_td_confirm_btn') {
            userloc_td_zip = $('#top_location_popup #userloc_td_zip').val();
        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'userloc_tm_confirm_btn') {
            userloc_td_zip = $('#toploc_section #userloc_tm_zip').val();
        }
        
        if(userloc_td_zip.trim() == '')
        {
            alert('Please enter a valid zip/postal code');
            return;
        }
        $("#loader").show();
        $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: '/ajax/_global/user_location',
                data: "mode=confirm_zip&zip="+userloc_td_zip,
                dataType: "json",
                success: function(data)
                {
                    if (data.status == "OK") {
                        if(clckd_elm_id == 'userloc_td_confirm_btn') {
                            $('#top_location_popup').modal('hide');
                            $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
                        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'userloc_tm_confirm_btn') {
                            $(".closeWindow").trigger("click");
                        }
                        $('#prddtl-find-store #findStoreZip').val(userloc_td_zip);
                        $('.find_sop #zip').val(userloc_td_zip);
                        $('.find_store #zip').val(userloc_td_zip);
                        if(prevent_track_confirm_btn_click != 'yes'){
                            trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Geo-Location Popup', 'confirm button click', userloc_td_zip);
                        }
                        $('#top_location_popup').addClass('ignore-track');
                    }
                }
            }).done(function(data) {
                $("#loader").hide();
            });
    });
    
    
   
    
    $('body').on('click', '#userloc_td_usemy_loc, #userloc_tm_usemy_loc', function (el){
        
        var clckd_elm_id = $(this).attr('id');
        if (navigator.geolocation)
        {
            var self = this;
            navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(function(position) {
                if(clckd_elm_id == 'userloc_td_usemy_loc') {
                    var zip = showMyPositionLocalNew(position, self,function(data) {
                        $('#top_location_popup #userloc_td_zip').val(data.location.zip);
                        prevent_track_confirm_btn_click = 'yes';
                        trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Geo-Location Popup', 'update current location link click', data.location.zip);
                        $('#top_location_popup').addClass('ignore-track');
                        $("#userloc_td_confirm_btn").click();
                    });
                } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'userloc_tm_usemy_loc') {
                    var zip = showMyPositionLocalNew(position, self,function(data) {
                        $('#toploc_section #userloc_tm_zip').val(data.location.zip);
                        prevent_track_confirm_btn_click = 'yes';
                        trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Geo-Location Popup', 'update current location link click', data.location.zip);
                        $('#top_location_popup').addClass('ignore-track');
                        $("#userloc_tm_confirm_btn").click();
                    });
                }
            }, showMyErrorLocal);
        } else {
            alert("Your browser does not support geo location.");
        }

        //Temp
        /*

    $('body').on('click', '#prddtl-find-store #findStoreSubmit, #findstore_popup_outer #findStorePopSubmit, #findstore_popup_outer #fs_m_upzipbx_submit, #findstore_popup_outer #fndstorebackarw', function (el){
        var clckd_elm_id = $(this).attr('id');
        var show_schedule_viewing_in_store_btn = 0;
        if($('#show_schedule_viewing_in_store_btn').length) {
            show_schedule_viewing_in_store_btn = $('#show_schedule_viewing_in_store_btn').val();
        }
        
        var userloc_fs_zip = '';
        if(clckd_elm_id == 'findStoreSubmit') {
            userloc_fs_zip = $('#prddtl-find-store #findStoreZip').val();
        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'findStorePopSubmit') {
            userloc_fs_zip = $('#findstore_popup_outer #findStorePopZip').val();
        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'fs_m_upzipbx_submit') {
            userloc_fs_zip = $('#findstore_popup_outer #fs_m_upzipbx_zip').val();
        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'fndstorebackarw') {
            userloc_fs_zip = $('#prddtl-find-store #findStoreZip').val();
        }
        
        if(userloc_fs_zip.trim() == '') {
            alert('Please enter a valid zip/postal code');
            return;
        }
        
        
        var w = document.body.clientWidth;
        var uloc_device = 'desk';
        if (w > 991) {
            uloc_device = 'desk';
        } else {
            uloc_device = 'mob';
        }
        
        productPageMobileScrollSetting(uloc_device);    

        var data_str = '';
        if(clckd_elm_id == 'findStoreSubmit') {
            data_str = 'mode=find_store_popup&zip='+userloc_fs_zip+'&uloc_device='+uloc_device+'&show_schedule_viewing_in_store_btn='+show_schedule_viewing_in_store_btn;
        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'findStorePopSubmit') {
            data_str = 'mode=find_store_popup&zip='+userloc_fs_zip+'&uloc_device='+uloc_device+'&inner_contents=1&show_schedule_viewing_in_store_btn='+show_schedule_viewing_in_store_btn;
        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'fs_m_upzipbx_submit') {
            data_str = 'mode=find_store_popup&zip='+userloc_fs_zip+'&uloc_device='+uloc_device+'&inner_contents=1';
        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'fndstorebackarw') {
            data_str = 'mode=find_store_popup&zip='+userloc_fs_zip+'&uloc_device='+uloc_device;
        }
        
        $("#loader").show();
        $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: '/ajax/_global/user_location',
                data: data_str,
                dataType: "json",
                success: function(data)
                {
                    if (data.status == "OK") {
                        
                        if(clckd_elm_id == 'findStoreSubmit') {
                            $('.show_back_bg').remove();
                            $('.sidenavbg').remove();
                            $('#findstore_popup_outer').remove();
                            $('body').append(data.html);
                            trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Store Locator Leads', 'find store click on product page', userloc_fs_zip);
                        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'findStorePopSubmit') {
                            $('#findstore_popup_outer .loc_l-inner').html(data.html);
                        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'fs_m_upzipbx_submit') {
                            $('#findstore_popup_outer #fs_m_store_res').html(data.html);
                        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'fndstorebackarw') {
                            //$('#findstore_popup_outer .sttoploc_section').html(data.html);
                            var htmlcontent_1 = $('.sttoploc_section', data.html).html();
                            $('#findstore_popup_outer .sttoploc_section').html(htmlcontent_1);
                        }
                        var e = $("#store-hoz-slider");
                        e.children().each( function( index ) {
                            $(this).attr( 'data-position', index );
                        });
                        e.owlCarousel({
                                loop: false,
                                margin:10,
                                responsiveClass: true,
                                nav: true,
                                dots:false,
                                mouseDrag: true,
                                items: 1,
                                navText: ['<i class="fa fa-angle-left"></i>', '<i class="fa fa-angle-right"></i>'],
                                responsive: {
                                  0: {items: 1, stagePadding:50},
                                  480: {items: 2, stagePadding:50},
                                  768: {items: 2, stagePadding:50},
                                  992: {items: 2, stagePadding:50}
                                }
                            }).on("changed.owl.carousel", function(s) {
                                var i = s.item.index;
                                $("#findstore_popup_outer #store-hoz-slider .owl-item li").removeClass('active');
                                $("#findstore_popup_outer #store-hoz-slider").find("[data-fdstore-id='" + i + "']").addClass('active');
                            });
                            
                            $('#store-hoz-slider').off('click').on('click', '.owl-item>li', function (el){
                                if(!$(this).hasClass('active')) {
                                    e.trigger("to.owl.carousel", [ $(this).attr( 'data-position'), 500, !0 ]);
                                    e.find(".owl-item li").removeClass('active');
                                    e.find(".owl-item").eq($(this).attr( 'data-position')).children('li').addClass("active");
                                }
                            });
                        
                            
                        if(clckd_elm_id == 'findStoreSubmit') {
                            setTimeout(function(){
                                $("#findstore_popup_outer").addClass("sideNavMob");
                                $(".sidenavbg").show();
                            }, 50); 
                        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'findStorePopSubmit') {
                            $('#prddtl-find-store #findStoreZip').val(userloc_fs_zip);  
                            $('.find_sop #zip').val(userloc_fs_zip);
                            $('.find_store #zip').val(userloc_fs_zip);
                        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'fs_m_upzipbx_submit') {
                            $('#prddtl-find-store #findStoreZip').val(userloc_fs_zip);  
                            $('.find_sop #zip').val(userloc_fs_zip);
                            $('.find_store #zip').val(userloc_fs_zip);
                        }
                        AddProductPagePopMobileScroll();
                    }
                }
            }).done(function(data) {
                $("#loader").hide();
            });
    });
    
    $(document.body).off('click', '#findstore_popup_outer .closeWindow').on('click', '#findstore_popup_outer .closeWindow', function(e)
    {   
        $(".sidenavbg").hide();
        $("#findstore_popup_outer").removeClass("sideNavMob");

    $('body').on('click', '#prddtl-find-store #prddtl-use-my-loc, #findstore_popup_outer #findStorePopUseMyLoc', function (el){
        var clckd_elm_id = $(this).attr('id');
        if (navigator.geolocation)
        {
            var self = this;
            navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(function(position) {
                if(clckd_elm_id == 'prddtl-use-my-loc') {
                    var zip = showMyPositionLocalNew(position, self,function(data) {
                        $('#prddtl-find-store #findStoreZip').val(data.location.zip);
                        $('.find_sop #zip').val(data.location.zip);
                        $('.find_store #zip').val(data.location.zip);
                        $("#prddtl-find-store .findStoreSubmit").click();
                    });
                } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'findStorePopUseMyLoc') {
                    var zip = showMyPositionLocalNew(position, self,function(data) {
                        var w = document.body.clientWidth;
                        if (w > 991)
                        {
                            $('#findstore_popup_outer #findStorePopZip').val(data.location.zip);
                            $('#prddtl-find-store #findStoreZip').val(data.location.zip);   
                            $('.find_sop #zip').val(data.location.zip);
                            $('.find_store #zip').val(data.location.zip);
                            $("#findstore_popup_outer .findStorePopSubmit").click();
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            $('#findstore_popup_outer #fs_m_upzipbx_zip').val(data.location.zip);
                            $('#prddtl-find-store #fs_m_upzipbx_zip').val(data.location.zip);   
                            $("#findstore_popup_outer .fs_m_upzipbx_submit").click();
                        }
                    });
                }

            }, showMyErrorLocal);
        } else {
            alert("Your browser does not support geo location.");
        }

    });

    $('body').on('click', '#book-appointment-btn, #findstore_popup_outer #book_appmntpop_submit,#findstore_popup_outer #bookappbackarw', function (el){
        var clckd_elm_id = $(this).attr('id');
        var w = document.body.clientWidth;
        var uloc_device = 'desk';
        if (w > 991) {
            uloc_device = 'desk';
        } else {
            uloc_device = 'mob';
        }
        
         productPageMobileScrollSetting(uloc_device);
         
        var data_str = 'mode=book_appointment_popup&uloc_device='+uloc_device;
        if(clckd_elm_id == 'book-appointment-btn') {
            data_str = 'mode=book_appointment_popup&uloc_device='+uloc_device;
        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'book_appmntpop_submit') {
            var userloc_fs_zip = $('#findstore_popup_outer #book_appmntpop_zip').val();
            if(userloc_fs_zip.trim() == '')
            {
                alert('Please enter a valid zip/postal code');
                return;
            }
            data_str = 'mode=book_appointment_popup&zip='+userloc_fs_zip+'&uloc_device='+uloc_device+'&inner_contents=1';
        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'bookappbackarw') {
            data_str = 'mode=book_appointment_popup&uloc_device='+uloc_device;
        }
        
        
        $("#loader").show();
        $.ajax({
                type: 'POST',
                url: '/ajax/_global/user_location',
                data: data_str,
                dataType: "json",
                success: function(data)
                {
                    if (data.status == "OK") {
                        if(clckd_elm_id == 'book-appointment-btn') {
                            $('.show_back_bg').remove();
                            $('.sidenavbg').remove();
                            $('#findstore_popup_outer').remove();
                            $('body').append(data.html);
                            trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Store Locator Leads', 'book appointment click on product page', $('#findStoreZip').val());
                        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'book_appmntpop_submit') {
                            $('#findstore_popup_outer .loc_l-inner .inner').html(data.html);
                        } else if(clckd_elm_id == 'bookappbackarw') {
                            //var html = $(data);
                            var htmlcontent_1 = $('.sttoploc_section', data.html).html();
                            $('#findstore_popup_outer .sttoploc_section').html(htmlcontent_1);
                            //$('.show_back_bg').remove();
                            //$('.sidenavbg').remove();
                            //$('#findstore_popup_outer').remove();
                            //$('body').append(data.html);
                        }
                        
                        if (w <= 991)
                        {
                            var e = $("#store-hoz-slider");
                            e.children().each( function( index ) {
                                $(this).attr( 'data-position', index );
                            });
                            e.owlCarousel({
                                loop: false,
                                margin:10,
                                responsiveClass: true,
                                nav: true,
                                dots:false,
                                mouseDrag: true,
                                items: 1,
                                navText: ['<i class="fa fa-angle-left"></i>', '<i class="fa fa-angle-right"></i>'],
                                responsive: {
                                  0: {items: 1, stagePadding:50},
                                  480: {items: 2, stagePadding:50},
                                  768: {items: 2, stagePadding:50},
                                  992: {items: 2, stagePadding:50}
                                }
                            }).on("changed.owl.carousel", function(s) {
                                var i = s.item.index;
                                $("#findstore_popup_outer #store-hoz-slider .owl-item li").removeClass('active');
                                $("#findstore_popup_outer #store-hoz-slider").find("[data-fdstore-id='" + i + "']").addClass('active');
                            });
                            
                            
                            $('#store-hoz-slider').off('click').on('click', '.owl-item>li', function (el){
                                if(!$(this).hasClass('active')) {
                                    e.trigger("to.owl.carousel", [ $(this).attr( 'data-position'), 500, !0 ]);
                                    e.find(".owl-item li").removeClass('active');
                                    e.find(".owl-item").eq($(this).attr( 'data-position')).children('li').addClass("active");
                                }
                            });
                            
                            if(userloc_fs_zip != undefined) {
                                $('#prddtl-find-store #fs_m_upzipbx_zip').val(userloc_fs_zip);  
                            }
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            $('#findstore_popup_outer #store-hoz-slider li').off('click').on('click', function(e) {
                                $("#findstore_popup_outer #store-hoz-slider li").removeClass('active');
                                $(this).addClass('active');
                            });
                            if(userloc_fs_zip != undefined) {
                                $('#prddtl-find-store #findStoreZip').val(userloc_fs_zip);  
                                $('.find_sop #zip').val(userloc_fs_zip);
                                $('.find_store #zip').val(userloc_fs_zip);
                            }
                            
                        }
                        
                        if(clckd_elm_id == 'book-appointment-btn') {
                            setTimeout(function(){
                                $("#findstore_popup_outer").addClass("sideNavMob");
                                $(".sidenavbg").show();
                            }, 50); 
                        }
                        if(clckd_elm_id == 'bookappbackarw') {
                            setTimeout(function(){
                                $("#findstore_popup_outer").addClass("sideNavMob");
                                $(".sidenavbg").show();
                            }, 50); 
                        }
                        
                        AddProductPagePopMobileScroll();
                    }
                }
            }).done(function(data) {
                $("#loader").hide();
            });
    });

    // Book Appointment Step 2
    $('body').on('click', '#findstore_popup_outer #book_appmnt_nxt_btn, #findstore_popup_outer .findStorePopBookApp', function (el){
        
        var w = document.body.clientWidth;
        var uloc_device = 'desk';
        if (w > 991) {
            uloc_device = 'desk';
        } else {
            uloc_device = 'mob';
        }
        
        productPageMobileScrollSetting(uloc_device);

        var retId = '';
        var addId = '';
        var sourcemd = '';
        if($(this).hasClass('book_appmnt_nxt_btn')) {
            retId = $("#findstore_popup_outer #store-hoz-slider li.active").attr('data-ret');
            addId = $("#findstore_popup_outer #store-hoz-slider li.active").attr('data-sto');
            sourcemd = 'appointment';
        } else {
            retId = $(this).attr('data-ret');
            addId = $(this).attr('data-sto');
            sourcemd = 'findstore';
        }
        
        $("#loader").show();
        $.ajax({
                type: 'POST',
                url: '/ajax/_global/user_location',
                data: 'mode=book_appointment_popup_step_2&uloc_device='+uloc_device+'&retId='+retId+'&addId='+addId+'&itemCode='+$('#book-appointment-btn').attr('data-itemcode')+'&sourcemd='+sourcemd,
                dataType: "json",
                success: function(data)
                {
                    if (data.status == "OK") {
                        $('#findstore_popup_outer .sttoploc_section').html(data.html);
                        AddProductPagePopMobileScroll();
                        
                        if($(this).hasClass('findStorePopBookApp')) {
                            if($('#book_appmntpop_zip').length > 0){
                                var userloc_fs_zip = $('#book_appmntpop_zip').val();
                            }
                            if($('#book_appmntpop_zip').length > 0){
                                var userloc_fs_zip = $('#book_appmntpop_zip').val();
                            }
                            trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Store Locator Leads', 'book appointment click on product page', userloc_fs_zip);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }).done(function(data) {
                $("#loader").hide();
            });
    });

    $('body').on('click', '#findstore_popup_outer .book_appmnt_submit_btn', function (el){
        $("#productRequestForm").unbind('validate').validate({
            rules: {

                },

                    required: true
                },

                        }
                    },
                },
                email: {
                    required: {
                        depends: function(element) {
                          return ($("input[name='contactMethod']:checked").val() == 'Email');
                        }
                    },
                },
                
            }
        });
        
        $('input[name="contactMethod"]').on('change', function() {
            $('#productRequestForm #phone').valid();
            $('#productRequestForm #email').valid();
        });
        
        var w = document.body.clientWidth;
        var uloc_device = 'desk';
        if (w > 991) {
            uloc_device = 'desk';
        } else {
            uloc_device = 'mob';
        }
        
        //$('#productRequestForm').submit(function (e) {
            //e.preventDefault();
            //e.stopPropagation()
            
            if (!$("#productRequestForm").valid()) return false;
            if (typeof pintrk == 'function') {
                pintrk('track', 'lead', {});
            }
            
            $("#loader").show();
            $.post('/ajax/_global/privaterequestsave', $('#productRequestForm').serialize(), function (data) {
                
                trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Store Locator Leads', 'submitted appointment leads', $('#productRequestForm #zipcode').val());
                
                //Start GTM
                dataLayer.push({
                    'event': 'productscheduleviewing',
                    'virtualpage': window.location.pathname+'/view-in-store-submission',
                    'virtualtitle': 'product schedule viewing'
                });

                 var curlocation = window.location;
                 var live = curlocation.toString();

                if (live.indexOf("dev") <= 0) {
                    (function (w, d, s, l, i) {
                        w[l] = w[l] || [];
                        w[l].push({
                            'gtm.start': new Date().getTime(), event: 'gtm.js'
                        });

                            j = d.createElement(s), dl = l != 'dataLayer' ? '&l=' + l : '';
                        j.async = true;
                        j.src ='https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtm.js?id=' + i + dl;
                        f.parentNode.insertBefore(j, f);
                    })(window, document, 'script', 'dataLayer', 'GTM-WGPZ3WF');


                    //AJAX GOOGLE TRACKING//
                    (function(i,s,o,g,r,a,m){i['GoogleAnalyticsObject']=r;i[r]=i[r]||function(){
                            (i[r].q=i[r].q||[]).push(arguments)},i[r].l=1*new Date();a=s.createElement(o),
                        m=s.getElementsByTagName(o)

                    type: "POST",

                    {

                            var w = document.body.clientWidth;
                            if (w > 991) {

                            } else {

    function productPageMobileScrollSetting($device="desk"){
        
        //if($device=="mob"){            
            //$("body").css("overflow-y", "hidden");
            if(!$("body").hasClass("noscroll-vertical")) {
                $("body").addClass("noscroll-vertical");
            }
        //}
        $(document.body).off('click', '.closeWindow,.closebtn').on('click', '.closeWindow,.closebtn', function(e)
        {           
            //$("body").removeClass("prodmobscrhide");   
            //$("body").removeAttr("style")
            if($("body").hasClass("noscroll-vertical")) {
                $("body").removeClass("noscroll-vertical")
            }
        }); 
    }
    
    function AddProductPagePopMobileScroll(){
        var w = document.body.clientWidth;
        var uloc_device = 'desk';
        if (w > 991) {
            device = 'desk';
        } else {
            device = 'mob';
        }
        if(device=="mob"){
            var margval = 150;
            if($('.mobscrollenable').closest('.find-store-second-step').hasClass('find-store-second-step'))
            {
                margval = 0;
            }
            if($('.mobscrollenable').closest('.find-store-first-step').hasClass('find-store-first-step'))
            {
                margval = 100;
            }
            setTimeout(function(){ 
                if($('.mobscrollenable').height()+margval >= $(window.top).height())
                {
                    if(!$('.mobscrollenable').hasClass('mobscrollenabled')) {
                        $('.mobscrollenable').addClass('mobscrollenabled');
                        $('.mobscrollenable').overlayScrollbars({
                            className       : "os-theme-round-dark",
                            resize          : "both",
                            sizeAutoCapable : true,
                            paddingAbsolute : false
                        });
                        if($('.mobscrollenable').closest('.book-appointment-second-step').hasClass('book-appointment-second-step'))
                        {
                            $('.mobscrollenable').addClass('mobscrollenabled3');
                        }
                    }
                }
            }, 500);
        }
        
        if(device=="desk"){
            
            if($('.deskscrollenable').closest('.find-store-first-step').hasClass('find-store-first-step') || $('.deskscrollenable').closest('.book-appointment-second-step').hasClass('book-appointment-second-step') || $('.deskscrollenable').closest('.book-appointment-first-step').hasClass('book-appointment-first-step'))
            {
                var margval = 150;
                if($('.deskscrollenable').closest('.find-store-first-step').hasClass('find-store-first-step'))
                {
                    margval = 150;
                }
                if($('.deskscrollenable').closest('.book-appointment-second-step').hasClass('book-appointment-second-step'))
                {
                    margval = 350;
                }
                if($('.deskscrollenable').closest('.book-appointment-first-step').hasClass('book-appointment-first-step'))
                {
                    margval = 50;
                }

                setTimeout(function(){ 
                    if($('.deskscrollenable').height()+margval > $(window.top).height())
                    {
                        if(!$('.deskscrollenable').hasClass('deskscrollenabled')) {
                            $('.deskscrollenable').addClass('deskscrollenabled');
                            $('.deskscrollenable').overlayScrollbars({
                                className       : "os-theme-round-dark",
                                resize          : "both",
                                sizeAutoCapable : true,
                                paddingAbsolute : false
                            });
                        }
                    }
                }, 500);
                
                
            }
        }
    }

    $(window).bind("orientationchange", function() {
        AddProductPagePopMobileScroll();
    });
    
    // Book An Appointment End

    // Store analytics event impression start
    $(document.body).on('click','.analyticsEventLogButton', function(e) {   
        if($(this).data('action') == 'phone')
        {
            if($('#findStorePopZip').length > 0) {
                var userloc_fs_zip = $('#findStorePopZip').val();
            } else if($('#findStoreZip').length > 0) {
                var userloc_fs_zip = $('#findStoreZip').val();
            }
            trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Store Locator Leads', 'call for availability click', userloc_fs_zip);
        }
        if($(this).data('action') == 'direction')
        {
            if($('#findStorePopZip').length > 0) {
                var userloc_fs_zip = $('#findStorePopZip').val();
            } else if($('#findStoreZip').length > 0) {
                var userloc_fs_zip = $('#findStoreZip').val();
            }
            trackGeoLocFindStoreBookAppointmentEvents('Store Locator Leads', 'direction click', userloc_fs_zip);
        }
        var sRetaillerCode = $(this).data('retcode');
        var sRetailler = $(this).data('ret');
        var sStore = $(this).data('sto');
        var sAction = $(this).data('action');       
        $.post('/ajax/_global/storelog_analytics_event', {logRetailerCode: sRetaillerCode, logRetailer: sRetailler, logStore: sStore, logAction: sAction});
    });
    // Store analytics event impression end
    
    // Find A Store Detail Page End
});

function showViedoPlayer(_dialog, video)
{
    jwplayer("videoElement")
        .setup(
        {
            primary : "html5",
            file : video,
            width : 320,
            height : 320,
            autostart : true,
            repeat : true,
            controlbar : "none",
            dock : false,
            backcolor : 'transparent',
            wmode : 'transparent',
            skin :  ESHOP_CONFIG.videoPlayerSkin
        });
}

function showDialog(sMessage) {
	$('#progressmodal .close').remove();
	$('#progressmodal .modal-header').append('<button type="button" class="close closemodal sendclose" data-dismiss="modal" aria-hidden="true"></button>');
	$('#progressmodal .modal-body').html(sMessage);
	$('#progressmodal').modal({backdrop: 'static',keyboard: false }).show();	
}

var ESHOP_CONFIG = {
    isEmbed : function ()
    {
        try
        {
            return window.self !== window.top;
        } catch (e)
        {
            return true;
        }
    },
    videoPlayerSkin : "https://cdn-localassets.gabrielny.com/jwplayer/skins/five.xml"
};


$(function() 
{

  $(".btnGotoLogin").on('click', function (e) 
  {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        var address = $(this).attr('data-path');

        if(address!="")
        {
            document.location.href = address;
        }
        else
        {
            document.location.href = 'https://www.gabrielny.com';
        }
    });
});

function processFavListAddForm()
{

    $("#favTitle").keyup(function() {

       $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
       $('#modal-category-character').remove();

        if ($("#favTitle").val().length > 12)
	{
                $(str).modal();
		$("#favTitle").val($("#favTitle").val().substring(0, 11));
		$("#favTitle").focus();
	}
    });

  $("#favTitle").keydown(function() {

      $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
      $('#modal-category-character').remove();
        if ($("#favTitle").val().length > 12)
	{
                $(str).modal();
		$("#favTitle").val($("#favTitle").val().substring(0, 11));
		$("#favTitle").focus();
	}

  });

    $(document.body).off('click', '#btnSubmit').on('click', '#btnSubmit', function(e)
    {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();

        var str='<div id="modal-select-category" class="modal fade" tabindex="-1" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="sm_popup" aria-hidden="true">';
            str+='<div class="modal_vah">';
            str+='<div class="modal-dialog modal-sm">';
            str+='<div class="modal-content">';
            str+='<div class="modal-header tac" style="border:1px solid #eaedf2;">';
            str+='<button type="button" class="close" data-dismiss="modal" aria-hidden="true"></button>';
            str+='<h3 class="modal-title">Wish List Name</h3>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='<div class="modal-body" style="background-color:#eaedf2;">';
            str+='<div class="modal-favorite" id="favorite-form-content">';
            str+='<div class="p10">Enter Wish List Name</div>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='</div>';


        if($("#favTitle").val()=="")
        {
            $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
            $('#modal-select-category').remove();
            $(str).modal();
           return false;
        }


        $.getJSON("/ajax/account/favorites", {
            "mode": "addCat",
            "favTitle": $("#favTitle").val(),
            "favStatus": $("#favStatus").val(),
            "favItem": $("#favItemCode").val()
        },
        function(data)
        {
            if (data.status == "ok")
            {
                var tmplistName=" Added to your wishlist";
                if(data.listname!=""){
                    //tmplistName=" Added to your "+data.listname;
                    tmplistName=" Added to your wishlist";
                }
                $('#favorite-form-content').html(tmplistName);
                //$('#favorite-form-content').html("Removed from your wishlist"+tmplistName);
                setTimeout(function () { location.reload(); }, 2000);
            }
            else
            {
               $('#favorite-form-content').html("An unknown error has occurred");

               var close = setInterval(function() {
                                        $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
                                        $('#modal-add-to-favorite').modal('hide');
                                        $('#modal-add-to-favorite').data('modal', null);

                                        clearInterval(close);
                                }, 2000);

            }
        });
    });
}

					'mode': 'addMulItem',
					'favLink': favlink,
					'item': sItem,

                            var tmplistCatName=" Added to your wishlist";
                                if(data.addedCatName!=""){
                                    //tmplistCatName=" Added to your "+data.addedCatName+" Wishlist";
                                    tmplistName=" Added to your "+data.addedCatName;
                                }

							var str='<div id="modal-delete-product" class="modal fade" tabindex="-1" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="sm_popup"  aria-hidden="true">';

							str+='<div class="modal-header tac" style="border:1px solid #eaedf2;">';

							str+='<div class="modal-body" style="background-color:#eaedf2;">';

							str+='<div class="p10">'+tmplistCatName+'</div>';
							str+='</div>';
							str+='</div>';
							str+='</div>';
							str+='</div>';
							str+='</div>';
							str+='</div>';
							$(str).modal();

                                                        $(document.body).off('click', '#favorite-close').on('click', '#favorite-close', function(e)
                                                        { //window.location.reload();
                                                         });
						}
				 });

		 }
		 else
		 {
			var str='<div id="modal-delete-product" class="modal fade" tabindex="-1" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="sm_popup" aria-hidden="true">';
				str+='<div class="modal_vah">';
				str+='<div class="modal-dialog modal-sm">';

function moveProduct()
{

    $("#favTitle").keyup(function() {

       //$('.modal-backdrop').remove();
       $('#modal-category-character').remove();

        if ($("#favTitle").val().length > 12)
	{
                $(str).modal();
		$("#favTitle").val($("#favTitle").val().substring(0, 11));
		$("#favTitle").focus();
	}
    });

      //$('.modal-backdrop').remove();
      $('#modal-category-character').remove();
        if ($("#favTitle").val().length > 12)

                $(str).modal();

		$("#favTitle").focus();

		 $("#tab-content-"+menu_id).fadeIn();
	});
	$(document.body).off('click', '.movFavList').on('click', '.movFavList', function(e){

    $(document.body).off('click', '#btnSubmitMul').on('click', '#btnSubmitMul', function(e)
    {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();

        var checkStr='';
        var cntCheck=0;

        var str='<div id="modal-select-category" class="modal fade" tabindex="-1" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="sm_popup" aria-hidden="true">';
            str+='<div class="modal_vah">';
            str+='<div class="modal-dialog modal-sm">';
            str+='<div class="modal-content">';
            str+='<div class="modal-header tac" style="border:1px solid #eaedf2;">';
            str+='<button type="button" class="close" data-dismiss="modal" aria-hidden="true"></button>';
            str+='<h3 class="modal-title">Wish List Name</h3>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='<div class="modal-body" style="background-color:#eaedf2;">';
            str+='<div class="modal-favorite" id="favorite-form-content">';
            str+='<div class="p10">Enter Wish List Name</div>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='</div>';
            str+='</div>';


        if($("#favTitle").val()=="")
        {
           $('#modal-select-category').remove();
           $(str).modal();
           return false;
        }


            $('.bxRadio').each(function()
            {
                if($(this).is(":checked"))
                {
                    cntCheck++;
                    checkStr+=","+$(this).data('value');
                }

            });

        if(cntCheck>0)
        {
            checkStr=checkStr.substring(1);
             $.getJSON("/ajax/account/favorites", {
            "mode": "addCatMul",
            "favTitle": $("#favTitle").val(),
            "favStatus": $("#favStatus").val(),
            "favItem": checkStr
             },
            function(data)
            {
                if (data.status == "ok")
                {

                    $('#favorite-form-content').html("Removed from your wishlist");

                    setTimeout(function () { location.reload(); }, 2000);
                }
                else
                {
                   $('#favorite-form-content').html("An unknown error has occurred");


                   var close = setInterval(function() {
                            $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
                            $('#modal-add-to-favorite').modal('hide');
                            $('#modal-add-to-favorite').data('modal', null);

                            clearInterval(close);
                    }, 2000);

                }
            });
        }
    });


}

function showWait(message) {
	var loadimg = $('#loading-image').attr('src');
	$('#progressmodal .modal-body').html(message+'<br><img src="' + loadimg + '" alt="loading..." class="loadimgcls" />');
	$(".quickview-w").css("width", "300");
	$('#progressmodal').modal({
		backdrop: 'static',
		keyboard: false
	}).show();
}

function hideWait() {
    $('#progressmodal').modal('hide');
}

//For Floater Button
$(document).ready(function () {
	w = document.body.clientWidth;
	adjustFolterButton();
});
$(window).bind("resize orientationchange", function() {
	w = document.body.clientWidth;
	adjustFolterButton();
});
function adjustFolterButton(){
	if (w <= 767){
	  $(function($){
			$('.right-drawer-btn').click(function() {
				var it = $(this).data('it') || 1;
				switch ( it ){
					case 1 :
						$(this).parent().animate({bottom:'0px'}, {queue:false, duration: 300});
						break;
					case 2 :
						$(this).parent().animate({bottom:'-105px'}, {queue: false, duration: 300});
						break;
				}
				it++;
				if(it > 2) it = 1;
				$(this).data('it', it);
			})
		});
	}
}

    j.csstransitions() && (a.support.transition = new String(f("transition")), a.support.transition.end = i.transition.end[a.support.transition]), j.cssanimations() && (a.support.animation = new String(f("animation")), a.support.animation.end = i.animation.end[a.support.animation]), j.csstransforms() && (a.support.transform = new String(f("transform")), a.support.transform3d = j.csstransforms3d())
}(window.Zepto || window.jQuery, window, document);</script>
